2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unreal Engine 5.8 C++ project `JamGame` (engine at `F:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8`), automation tests `Jam.<Area>.<Case>`, pure `namespace <Feature>Rules` with `JAMGAME_API` free functions and a header line `Tests: Jam.<Area>.*`.
   - Code standard (`.claude/rules/cpp.md`): Epic style + `.clang-format` (tabs, Allman, braces always, ColumnLimit 140); English comments; folders `Public/<System>` and `Private/<System>` plus `Tests`; `Core` and `Sim` never include `View`, `UI`, `Audio` (ratchet lists `SIMULATION = ("Core","Sim")`, `PRESENTATION = ("View","UI","Audio")` in `Tools/Quality/check_code.py`; cycles between systems are also flagged); balance numbers in data; simulation randomness from a seeded stream; no `LogTemp`; soft thresholds (cpp 600 lines, function 60, nesting 3); the number of `Jam.` tests only grows.
   - **Architecture decided for the prototype** (recorded in `Tickets/INDEX.md`): the fight is a pure fixed-step (60 Hz) simulation on plain data, `FJamFight` advanced by `JamFightRules::Step`; flat 2D arena (sim X,Y = world X,Y); every fighter is driven by an `FJamIntent` (move, aim, buttons, choice) written by the player controller, the boss brain or the hero bot; a mechanic is a sim-side component with an owner and a steal is a change of owner in a ledger; the steal slowdown is a time scale inside the sim, not global time dilation.
   - Planned layering to avoid a `Game <-> View` cycle: the sim host `UJamFightSubsystem` (plain `UWorldSubsystem` with `Advance(Dt)` called from `AJamGameMode::Tick`) lives in `Sim`; camera mode and look yaw/pitch live in the camera rig (`View`), the controller (`Game`) writes into it; `Game` includes `View`, `UI`, `Sim`; `View` and `UI` include `Sim` only.
   - Planned balance loading: `FJamBalanceData` is a `USTRUCT` with zero defaults; values come from a flat `[JamBalance]` section of `Config/DefaultGame.ini`, loaded by reflection (`TFieldIterator<FProperty>`, `GConfig->GetString(Section, Name, Value, GGameIni)`, `ImportText_Direct`); camera numbers in a separate `FJamCameraSettings` struct in `View` with its own `[JamCamera]` section; tests use a fixed `JamTest::Balance()` fixture, and one test validates the real ini.
   - Planned input: keys polled in `AJamPlayerController::PlayerTick` (`IsInputKeyDown`, `WasInputKeyJustPressed`, `GetInputMouseDelta`, `DeprojectMousePositionToWorld`), no Enhanced Input assets; top-down uses `FInputModeGameAndUI` with a visible cursor, the other two use `FInputModeGameOnly`.
   - Planned view: everything spawned at runtime from basic shapes (`/Engine/BasicShapes/Sphere`, `Cylinder`, `Cube`, MIDs of `BasicShapeMaterial` with the vector parameter `Color`, verified in the asset), `AJamArenaView` with floor, directional light, sky atmosphere and sky light components; a blank `/Game/Maps/Arena` map created by an editor Python script; `+DirectoriesToAlwaysCook=(Path="/Engine/BasicShapes")` and the map in the packaging settings; HUD drawn with canvas in `AJamHUD`; fighter views interpolate between `PrevPos` and `Pos` with the accumulator alpha.
   - Engine API facts checked: `FProperty::ImportText_Direct(const TCHAR* Buffer, TNotNull<void*> PropertyPtr, UObject* OwnerObject, int32 PortFlags, FOutputDevice* ErrorText = GWarn)`; test flags `EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::ProductFilter` (CastleFight uses the same as `CFTest::Flags`); `JamGame.Target.cs` uses `BuildSettingsVersion.V7`, `EngineIncludeOrderVersion.Unreal5_8`; `JamGame.Build.cs` has `PublicIncludePaths` "JamGame" and depends on Core, CoreUObject, Engine, InputCore.
   - fal queue API: POST `https://queue.fal.run/<endpoint>` with header `Authorization: Key <FAL_KEY>`, poll `status_url`, GET `response_url`; endpoint `openai/gpt-image-2.6/flare/text-to-image` (also `/flare/edit`, `/sunburst/text-to-image`, `/sunburst/edit`); input fields `prompt`, `image_size` (preset or `{width,height}`), `quality` (default high), `reasoning_effort`, `num_images`, `output_format`, `background`.
   - Session log export: `Tools/Devlog/export_sessions.py` run by `Tools/Git/hooks/pre-commit`, redacts keys and emails; this session id is `c97397be-cf72-4ec9-ba97-3ec9a5e0a8bf` under slug `F--Unreal-Engine-UnrealProjects-CastleFight`.
   - rtk rewrites shell commands transparently; multi-line commit messages go through `git commit -F <file>`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unreal Engine 5.8 C++ project `JamGame` (engine at `F:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8`), automation tests `Jam.<Area>.<Case>`, pure `namespace <Feature>Rules` with `JAMGAME_API` free functions and a header line `Tests: Jam.<Area>.*`.
   - Code standard (`.claude/rules/cpp.md`): Epic style + `.clang-format` (tabs, Allman, braces always, ColumnLimit 140; clang-format puts short accessors on one line and indents `case` labels); English comments; folders `Public/<System>` and `Private/<System>` plus `Private/Tests`; `Core` and `Sim` never include `View`, `UI`, `Audio` (ratchet in `Tools/Quality/check_code.py`: `SIMULATION = ("Core","Sim")`, `PRESENTATION = ("View","UI","Audio")`, cycles flagged, `MAX_FILE_LINES = 600`, function 60 lines, nesting 3, no `LogTemp`); balance numbers in data; simulation randomness from a seeded stream; the number of `Jam.` tests only grows (baseline in `Tools/Quality/baseline.json`).
   - Architecture: the fight is a pure fixed-step (60 Hz) simulation on plain data, `FJamFight` advanced by `JamFightRules::Step`; flat 2D arena (sim X,Y = world X,Y); every fighter is driven by an `FJamIntent` (held-button semantics); a mechanic will be a sim-side component with an owner; the steal slowdown will be a time scale inside the sim.
   - Frame order is owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`: read the player's intent, `UJamFightSubsystem::Advance`, sync views, sync camera. Views have no tick of their own. The game mode adds the player controller as a tick prerequisite. The player has no pawn (`RestartPlayer` overridden empty, `DefaultPawnClass = nullptr`), the view target is `AJamCameraRig`.
   - Layering: `Game` includes `Sim`, `View`, `UI`; `View` and `UI` include `Core`/`Sim` only; the HUD gets camera-dependent state pushed in by the game mode (`bCrosshair`, `CameraLabel`).
   - Balance and camera numbers: `USTRUCT`s with zero defaults (`FJamBalanceData`, `FJamCameraSettings`), loaded by reflection from flat ini sections `[JamBalance]` and `[JamCamera]` of `Config/DefaultGame.ini` through `JamConfig::Load<T>(Section, &Missing)` (`TFieldIterator<FProperty>`, `GConfig->GetString(..., GGameIni)`, `ImportText_Direct`). Tests use the fixed fixture `JamTest::Balance()`; two tests validate the real ini.
   - Input: keys polled in the controller (`IsInputKeyDown`, `WasInputKeyJustPressed`, `GetInputMouseDelta`, `DeprojectMousePositionToWorld`), no Enhanced Input assets; top-down uses `FInputModeGameAndUI` (lock always, cursor not hidden during capture) with a visible cursor, the other two use `FInputModeGameOnly`.
   - View: everything spawned at runtime from `/Engine/BasicShapes/{Sphere,Cylinder,Cube}` and MIDs of `BasicShapeMaterial` (vector parameter `Color`) through the `JamShapes` helper (static `ConstructorHelpers::FObjectFinder`, first call must be in a constructor); views interpolate between `PrevPos` and `Pos` with the accumulator alpha.
   - Unity builds: names in anonymous namespaces must be unique across cpp files of the module.
   - Engine facts checked: the generic `TestEqual<ValueType>` template exists in `AutomationTest.h` (compares with `!=`, works for enum classes and `FVector2D`), with both `const TCHAR*` and `const FString&` first arguments; `FMath::ClosestPointOnSegment2D` compiles for `FVector2D`.
   - fal queue API and `Tools/Art/fal_images.py` (ledger, 5 USD cap) from the first half hour; butler at `F:\Tools\butler\butler.exe` (v15.32.0, not on PATH, key `BUTLER_API_KEY` in `.env`, never printed).
   - Session log export: `Tools/Devlog/export_sessions.py` run by the pre-commit hook, redacts keys and emails; this session id is `c97397be-cf72-4ec9-ba97-3ec9a5e0a8bf` under slug `F--Unreal-Engine-UnrealProjects-CastleFight`.

Frame order owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`: intent (player or demo bot), `UJamFightSubsystem::Advance`, sync views, sync camera, demo end-of-frame. No pawn; view target is `AJamCameraRig`. Run with the editor binary in `-game` mode; `MSYS2_ARG_CONV_EXCL="*"` is needed so Git Bash does not mangle `/Game/...`. ffmpeg (winget) joins frames. A 26 s run takes about 90 s wall time.

   - Katana: `InArc(AttackerPos, AimDir, TargetPos, TargetRadius, Range, HalfAngleDeg)` (distance ≤ Range + TargetRadius and angle ≤ half arc + asin(TargetRadius/Dist)); `NextStrike(State, Tick)` returns 1..3 or 0 while busy; start sets HitTick = now + wind-up, BusyUntil = HitTick + recovery, ChainUntil = BusyUntil + chain window, steps forward `KatanaStep`; the hit lands once at HitTick; third strike uses `KatanaFinisherDamage`.
   - Shuriken: `IsExplosive(ThrowIndex, Every)` (index % every == 0), `ExplosionHits(Center, Radius, TargetPos, TargetRadius)`; per-owner counter; an explosive one deals the explosion damage in a radius on impact or at the end of its flight, hurting only the thrower's enemy.
   - Dash: `Direction(MoveInput, Facing)`, start/tick helpers, `IsDashing`, `IsInvulnerable`; per-step distance = DashDistance / Ticks(DashDuration) so the configured distance is exact; move intent ignored while dashing; arena clamp and push-out still apply; projectiles pass through an invulnerable target.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unreal Engine 5.8 C++ project `JamGame` (engine at `F:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8`), automation tests `Jam.<Area>.<Case>` with `JamTest::Flags`, pure `namespace <Feature>Rules` with `JAMGAME_API` free functions and a header comment line `Tests: Jam.<Area>.*`.
   - Code standard (`.claude/rules/cpp.md`): Epic style + `.clang-format` (run `clang-format -i --style=file <files>`; tabs, Allman, ColumnLimit 140); English comments; folders `Public/<System>`, `Private/<System>`, `Private/Tests`; `Core` and `Sim` never include `View`, `UI`, `Audio` (ratchet in `Tools/Quality/check_code.py`: file 600 lines, function 60 lines, nesting 3, no `LogTemp`, include cycles flagged); balance numbers in data; sim randomness from the seeded stream; the number of `Jam.` tests only grows (baseline in `Tools/Quality/baseline.json`, now 33; update with `python Tools/Quality/check_code.py --tests-log Saved/BuildCycle/AutoTests_last.log --update-baseline`; without `--tests-log` the count is not raised).
   - Architecture: the fight is a pure fixed-step (60 Hz) simulation on plain data, `FJamFight` advanced by `JamFightRules::Step(Fight, Intents, Balance)`; flat 2D arena; fighters driven by `FJamIntent` (held-button semantics). Frame order owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`: intent (player or demo bot), `UJamFightSubsystem::Advance`, sync views, sync camera, demo end-of-frame. No pawn; view target is `AJamCameraRig`. Layering: `Game` includes `Sim`, `View`, `UI`; `View`/`UI` include `Core`/`Sim` only.
   - Balance and camera numbers: `USTRUCT`s with zero defaults (`FJamBalanceData`, `FJamCameraSettings`) loaded by reflection from flat ini sections `[JamBalance]` and `[JamCamera]` of `Config/DefaultGame.ini` through `JamConfig::Load<T>(Section, &Missing)`. `JamBalanceRules::Problems` requires every float field positive and `ArenaRadius > 2 * (HeroRadius + BossRadius)`. Tests use the fixture `JamTest::Balance()`.
   - `JamSim::Ticks(Seconds)` = `max(1, RoundToInt32(Seconds * 60))`; `JamSim::Other(Side)`; `TJamPerSide<T>` indexed by `EJamSide`; `FJamIntents` = per-side intents.
   - Unity builds: names in anonymous namespaces must be unique across cpp files of the module (already used: `CountEvents` in JamShotHealthTest, `TestCamera` in JamCameraTest, `KatanaOrigin`/`KatanaFacing`/`AtAngle` in JamKatanaTest, `BossFight`/`NotSoon` in JamBossTest, `MakeFighter`/`AddEvent`/`MoveFighter`/`AdvanceProjectiles`/`ShootBall`/`HeroStartX`/`BossStartX` in JamFightRules.cpp, `RangeSoftness` in JamBotRules.cpp, `MaxStepsPerFrame` in the subsystem, `DemoRange`/`DemoCameraSeconds`/`CaptureFps`/`DemoCamera` in JamDemo.cpp, `HeroBeanColor`/`BossBeanColor`/`CameraName` in JamGameMode.cpp, `FlightHeight`/`HeroShotColor`/`BossShotColor` in JamProjectilesView.cpp, HUD constants `BossBarWidth` etc., `LookAimReach` in the controller).
   - Demo run and capture: command line `-JamDemo` (bot plays, camera cycles every 8 s), `-JamCapture=<folder>` (PNG per frame, fixed 30 fps step), `-JamDemoSeconds=<n>`. `Tools/Build/demo_clip.sh [seconds] [output.mp4]` runs it and joins frames with ffmpeg; `MSYS2_ARG_CONV_EXCL="*"` needed for `/Game/...` paths. A 26 s run takes about 90 s wall time. Warn the author before any run that opens a game window.
   - Multi-line file edits: small Python patch scripts written to the scratchpad with Write and run with `python <script> <root>` (each replacement asserts its match count), or the Edit tool (requires a prior Read tool call on the file; `cat` through Bash does not count); never heredocs.
   - rtk rewrites shell commands and may trim output; multi-line commit messages go through `git commit -F <file>`. Bash cwd resets to CastleFight after each call, so every command starts with `cd /f/Unreal_Engine/UnrealProjects/MegaJam2026 &&`. `.gitattributes`: sources/md/json/py/sh LF, `.ini` auto (DefaultEngine.ini CRLF, DefaultGame.ini LF), `*.uasset`, `*.umap`, images, `*.mp4` in LFS.
   - butler at `F:\Tools\butler\butler.exe` (key `BUTLER_API_KEY` in `.env`, never printed); fal tool `Tools/Art/fal_images.py` with a 5 USD cap ledger.

     ```cpp
     // in FJamIntent, after bDash:
     /** The Publisher's own attacks. Nobody else has them, the hero's are ignored. */
     bool bVolley = false;
     bool bSlam = false;

     enum class EJamMechanic : uint8 { Katana, Shuriken, Dash };
     namespace JamSim { inline constexpr int32 MechanicCount = 3; }
     struct FJamLedger
     {
     	EJamSide Owner[JamSim::MechanicCount] = { EJamSide::Hero, EJamSide::Hero, EJamSide::Hero };
     	bool Has(EJamSide Side, EJamMechanic Mechanic) const { return Owner[static_cast<int32>(Mechanic)] == Side; }
     };
     struct FJamKatanaState { int32 Strike = 0; int32 HitTick = 0; int32 BusyUntilTick = 0; int32 ChainUntilTick = 0; bool bHitPending = false; };
     struct FJamShurikenState { int32 ReadyTick = 0; int32 Throws = 0; };
     struct FJamDashState { FVector2D Dir = ZeroVector; float StepLength = 0.f; int32 EndTick = 0; int32 InvulnerableUntilTick = 0; int32 ReadyTick = 0; };
     // FJamFighter gained: FJamKatanaState Katana; FJamShurikenState Shuriken; FJamDashState Dash;
     struct FJamHazard { int32 Id = 0; EJamSide Owner = EJamSide::Boss; FVector2D Pos; float Radius = 0.f; float Damage = 0.f; int32 StartTick = 0; int32 HitTick = 0; };
     struct FJamBossState { int32 BusyUntilTick = 0; int32 VolleyReadyTick = 0; int32 SlamReadyTick = 0; };
     enum class EJamPhase : uint8 { Fight, HeroDead, Won };
     enum class EJamEvent : uint8 { ProjectileSpawned, ProjectileGone, Hit, Swing /*Amount = strike of the series*/, Explosion /*Amount = radius*/, Slam /*Amount = radius*/, Dash };
     // FJamFight gained: TArray<FJamHazard> Hazards; FJamLedger Ledger; FJamBossState Boss; EJamPhase Phase = EJamPhase::Fight;
     ```

Step-level expectations: a fresh fight from `Start` has the boss busy (`BusyUntilTick = Ticks(BossRecovery)`); a busy boss is not moved and starts no attack whatever his intent says; `bVolley` spawns `RoundToInt32(MemoCount)` memos of kind Memo owned by the boss at MemoSpeed, makes him busy, and repeats only after `Ticks(VolleyCooldown)`; `bSlam` puts one hazard at `Intent.Aim.Point` with radius SlamRadius, `HitTick = Tick + Ticks(SlamTelegraph)`; on the hit step a `Slam` event, SlamDamage to a hero whose centre is inside, hazard removed, boss not hurt and still busy (busy = telegraph + recovery); a hero at zero health sets `Phase = HeroDead`, after which `Tick` stops and nobody moves; a boss at zero sets `Phase = Won`.

   - `Public/Sim/JamDashRules.h` + cpp: `Direction(Move, Facing)`, `FJamDashState Start(Dir, Tick, Balance)` (Steps = Ticks(DashDuration), StepLength = DashDistance / Steps, EndTick, InvulnerableUntilTick, ReadyTick = max(EndTick, Tick + Ticks(DashCooldown))), `CanStart`, `IsDashing`, `IsInvulnerable`.
   - `Public/Sim/JamFightOps.h` + cpp: `struct FJamShot { Kind, Speed, Radius, Damage, Life, bExplosive }`; `AddEvent(Fight, Type, Side, ProjectileId, Pos, Amount)`; `bool Hurt(Fight, TargetSide, Amount, Pos, ProjectileId = 0)` (nothing while the target is in his dash window; adds `Hit`; sets `Phase` to `HeroDead`/`Won` at zero health); `Shoot(Fight, Owner, Dir, Shot)`; `Shift(Fight, Side, Delta, ArenaRadius)` (PushOut then ClampToArena).
   - `Public/Sim/JamKatanaRules.h` + cpp: `InArc(From, Facing, TargetPos, TargetRadius, Range, HalfArcDeg)`, `NextStrike(State, Tick)`, `Begin(Strike, Tick, Balance)`, `Damage(Strike, Balance)`, `Press(Fight, Side, Balance)` (starts a strike, steps forward by `Facing * KatanaStep` through `Shift`, `Swing` event with Amount = strike), `Land(Fight, Side, Balance)` (lands the pending strike on its `HitTick`).
   - `Public/Sim/JamShurikenRules.h` + cpp: `IsExplosive(ThrowIndex, Every)`, `ExplosionHits(Center, Radius, TargetPos, TargetRadius)`, `Throw(Fight, Side, Balance)`, `Explode(Fight, Shuriken, Balance)` (`Explosion` event with the radius, hurts only the thrower's enemy).
   - `Public/Sim/JamHazardRules.h` + cpp: `IsDue(Hazard, Tick)`, `Hits(Hazard, TargetPos)`, `float Progress(Hazard, float Tick)`, `Advance(Fight)` (due circles: removed, `Slam` event with Amount = radius, `Hurt` the owner's enemy if inside).
   - `Public/Sim/JamBossRules.h` + cpp: `FJamIntent Decide(Fight, Balance)` (aim at the hero; nothing while busy; slam if hero within `SlamRange` and ready; else volley if ready; else walk at the hero unless touching), `bool IsBusy(Fight)` (`Tick < Boss.BusyUntilTick`), `TArray<FVector2D> VolleyDirections(AimDir, Count, SpreadDeg)`, `Act(Fight, Intent, Balance)` (anonymous `StartSlam`: hazard at `Aim.Point`, `HitTick = Tick + Ticks(SlamTelegraph)`, `SlamReadyTick`, `BusyUntilTick = HitTick + Ticks(BossRecovery)`; `StartVolley`: `RoundToInt32(MemoCount)` memos along the fan around the boss's facing, `VolleyReadyTick`, `BusyUntilTick = Tick + Ticks(BossRecovery)`).
   - `Private/Sim/JamFightRules.cpp` rewritten, `Public/Sim/JamFightRules.h` gained `Play`. Step order now:
     ```cpp
     void JamFightRules::Step(FJamFight& Fight, const FJamIntents& Intents, const FJamBalanceData& Balance)
     {
     	Fight.Events.Reset();
     	if (Fight.Phase != EJamPhase::Fight) { return; } // time stands still after the end
     	for (Side : {Hero, Boss}) MoveFighter(Fight, Side, Intents[Side], Balance); // PrevPos, facing, dash start (Dash event), dash step or walk; a held boss does not move
     	AdvanceProjectiles(Fight, Balance); // passes through an invulnerable target; explosive -> Explode, hit -> Hurt, else ProjectileGone
     	JamHazardRules::Advance(Fight);
     	for (Side : {Hero, Boss}) UseButtons(Fight, Side, Intents[Side], Balance); // skipped for a busy boss; ActionFor -> Press / Throw / ShootBall
     	JamBossRules::Act(Fight, Intents[EJamSide::Boss], Balance);
     	for (Side : {Hero, Boss}) JamKatanaRules::Land(Fight, Side, Balance);
     	++Fight.Tick;
     }
     void JamFightRules::Play(FJamFight& Fight, const FJamIntent& HeroIntent, const FJamBalanceData& Balance); // Decide in the boss's seat, then Step
     ```
     `Start` sets `Fight.Boss.BusyUntilTick = JamSim::Ticks(Balance.BossRecovery)`.
   - `Public/Sim/JamBotRules.h` + cpp rewritten: `Kite(Fight, Side, Range)` unchanged; new `FJamIntent Play(const FJamFight&, EJamSide, const FJamBalanceData&)`: starts from `Kite` at `SlamRange * 1.5`; `bPrimary = !bHasKatana || in reach (KatanaRange + Target.Radius)`; `bSecondary = true`; `bDash = IsAboutToBeHit` (enemy projectile within 0.15 s closer than radii + 10); inside an enemy circle (radius + 30): move straight out (from the very centre: `-Aim.Dir`) with `bDash`; else with the katana and a busy boss: `Move = Aim.Dir`. Includes `Sim/JamBossRules.h`.
   - `Public/Sim/JamFightSubsystem.h` / `Private/Sim/JamFightSubsystem.cpp` (patched by `scratchpad/patch_host_b.py`): public `FJamIntent HeroIntent` replaces `FJamIntents Intents`; `Advance` calls `JamFightRules::Play(Fight, HeroIntent, Balance)` per step and appends `Fight.Events` to `FrameEvents`; new `void DebugSteal()` (first hero-owned mechanic goes to the boss; when none is left `Fight.Ledger = FJamLedger()`); `Restart()` resets `HeroIntent`. Getters: `GetFight()`, `GetBalance()`, `GetAlpha()`, `GetFrameEvents()`.

   - Architecture: the fight is a pure fixed-step (60 Hz) simulation on plain data, `FJamFight`; flat 2D arena; fighters driven by `FJamIntent` (held-button semantics). Frame order owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`: intent (player or demo bot), `UJamFightSubsystem::Advance` (calls `JamFightRules::Play` per step, collects `FrameEvents`), sync views, camera, demo end-of-frame. No pawn; view target is `AJamCameraRig`.
   - Every mechanic (Katana, Shuriken, Dash) has one owner in `FJamLedger`; both fighters carry the state of every mechanic; a steal is a change of owner. Hero attack buttons fall back to the weak ball; the dash button falls back to nothing; the boss gets nothing he was not given. The Publisher's brain (`JamBossRules::Decide`) writes the same intent a player writes; the boss is "busy" (stands, starts nothing) during an attack and its recovery.
   - Balance and camera numbers: `USTRUCT`s loaded by reflection from flat ini sections `[JamBalance]` and `[JamCamera]` of `Config/DefaultGame.ini` through `JamConfig::Load<T>(Section, &Missing)`; `JamBalanceRules::Problems` requires every float field positive (`Jam.Balance.IniIsCompleteAndValid`). Tests use the fixture `JamTest::Balance()` in `Private/Tests/JamTestHelpers.h` (also has `JamTest::Bean/Count/RunCounting`).    - Every mechanic (Katana, Shuriken, Dash) has one owner in `FJamLedger`; both fighters carry the state of every mechanic; a steal is a change of owner. Hero attack buttons fall back to the weak ball; the dash button falls back to nothing; the boss gets nothing he was not given. The Publisher's brain (`JamBossRules::Decide`) writes the same intent a player writes; the boss is "busy" (stands, starts nothing) during an attack and its recovery.

   - `Public/Core/JamTypes.h` (three edits done):
     ```cpp
     /** The Publisher's cuts: the ones already made and the one that waits for the player's answer. */
     struct FJamStealState
     {
     	/** Steals already made. */
     	int32 Done = 0;
     	/** The boss cannot drop below this health until the next steal is settled; 0 when no steal is left. */
     	float Floor = 0.f;
     	/** What he grabbed this time, left to right on the card. The player gives one away; with bTakesAll all of it goes. */
     	TArray<EJamMechanic> Offer;
     	bool bTakesAll = false;
     	/** Step on which the card opened. */
     	int32 StartTick = 0;
     	/** First step on which an answer counts. */
     	int32 OpenTick = 0;
     	/** Step on which the Publisher stops waiting and takes the left option. */
     	int32 EndTick = 0;
     };

     enum class EJamPhase : uint8
     {
     	Fight,
     	/** The choice card is up: the fight stands still until the steal is settled. */
     	Steal,
     	/** The boss is at zero and stunned, everything is back with the hero: one more hit ends it. */
     	Stunned,
     	HeroDead,
     	Won
     };
     ```
     `EJamEvent` gained `StealOpened` ("Amount is the number of options"), `StealResolved` ("Side is the new owner, Amount is the mechanic"), `Stunned`; `FJamFight` gained `FJamStealState Steal;` between `Boss` and `Phase`. `FJamIntent` already has `int32 Choice = -1`.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Block B verified and committed earlier. Measured: the bot kills the Publisher in about 12 seconds (target 3 to 4 minutes); balance pass belongs to block C / T-016, measured by the bot. Rough arithmetic: full-kit damage about 85 per second, the ball about 14 per second; BossHealth around 2400 to 3000 is the starting idea. The boss is weak against kiting (the bot holds 780 cm, memo fans are dodged by strafing); the stolen dash as a gap closer and the stolen shuriken should change that.
   - **Block C design, decided in this segment (only the types and balance fields are written so far):**
     - **No slow motion** (deviation from T-013, to be recorded in the ticket and DEVLOG): when a steal opens the fight freezes. `JamStealRules::Open` clears all projectiles and hazards and pending katana hits, both fighters stand (set `PrevPos = Pos` each step so the views do not jitter), the choice timer runs in sim ticks at normal time (`StealChoiceSeconds`), the tick counter keeps advancing. Reason: A/D are both move keys and choice keys, a clean stage makes the checkpoint trivial and the sim stays deterministic. The ticket test `Jam.Steal.FightSlowsDuringTheChoice` becomes something like `Jam.Steal.TheFightStandsDuringTheChoice`.
     - **`JamStealRules`** (new `Public/Sim/JamStealRules.h`, `Private/Sim/JamStealRules.cpp`): `inline constexpr int32 ThresholdCount = 3`; `ThresholdHealth(Index, Balance)` (share × BossHealth, 0 when no such steal); `ClampToThreshold(Health, Floor)` = max; `Offer(Ledger, bTakesAll, Stream)` (shuffle the hero's mechanics with the stream and take two; all of them in enum order when `bTakesAll`); `Open(Fight, Balance)` (`bTakesAll = Done == ThresholdCount - 1`; empty offer → just `++Done` and next floor; sets `StartTick`, `OpenTick = Tick + Ticks(StealChoiceLock)`, `EndTick = Tick + Ticks(StealChoiceSeconds)`, phase `Steal`, event `StealOpened`); `Advance(Fight, Choice, Balance)` (a valid choice at or after `OpenTick` resolves; at `EndTick` the left option, index 0); `Resolve(Fight, Choice, Balance)` (gives the option or all to the boss via `JamLoadoutRules::Give`, one `StealResolved` event per mechanic, `++Done`, `Floor = ThresholdHealth(Done)`, phase `Fight`, `Boss.BusyUntilTick = Tick + Ticks(BossRecovery)`); `TimeLeft(Steal, Tick)` 1 to 0 for the card timer. Tests: `Jam.Steal.HealthStopsAtTheThreshold`, `OneBigHitTriggersOnlyOneSteal`, `OffersTwoDifferentHeroMechanics`, `SameSeedSameOffer`, `LastThresholdTakesTheRest`, `ChosenMechanicGoesToTheBoss`, `TheOtherOneStays`, `ChoiceResolvesAtOnce` (after the lock), `TimeoutGivesTheDefault`, `NoDamageDuringTheChoice`, the standing-still test, a lock test.
     - **`JamFightOps::Hurt`**: no phase decisions any more; boss health is clamped to `Fight.Steal.Floor`; the hero is spared in `Stunned`; dash invulnerability as before; `Hit` event still added. Sketch:
       ```cpp
       const bool bSpared = TargetSide == EJamSide::Hero && Fight.Phase == EJamPhase::Stunned;
       const bool bInvulnerable = bSpared || JamDashRules::IsInvulnerable(Target.Dash, Fight.Tick);
       const float Floor = TargetSide == EJamSide::Boss ? Fight.Steal.Floor : 0.f;
       Target.Health = JamStealRules::ClampToThreshold(JamHealthRules::Apply(Target.Health, Amount, bInvulnerable), Floor);
       ```
     - **`JamOutcomeRules`** (new): pure `Next(Phase, HeroHealth, BossHealth, StealFloor, bBossWasHit)` (Stunned + boss hit → Won; from Fight: hero at 0 → HeroDead first, boss at 0 → Stunned, `Floor > 0 && BossHealth <= Floor` → Steal); `Settle(Fight, Balance)` called at the end of `Step` after `Land`: on Steal calls `JamStealRules::Open`, on Stunned returns every mechanic to the hero, clears projectiles/hazards/pending boss strike and adds the `Stunned` event. A hit on the stunned boss in a later step wins. Tests: `Jam.Outcome.HeroAtZeroIsALoss`, `BossAtZeroIsStunnedNotDead`, `EverythingReturnsOnStun`, `HitOnStunnedBossWins` in a new `JamOutcomeTest.cpp`; the two old fight-end tests in `JamBossTest.cpp` move there (the boss-at-zero test needs `F.Steal.Floor = 0`), and the header comment of `JamBossTest.cpp` loses "the end of the fight".
     - **`JamFightRules::Step`**: return on `HeroDead`/`Won`; in `Steal` only hold still, `JamStealRules::Advance(Fight, Intents[Hero].Choice, Balance)`, `++Tick`; otherwise the normal step with per-side numbers and `Settle` before `++Tick`. `Start` sets `Steal.Floor`. `IsBusy` grows to `Phase == Stunned || Tick < BusyUntilTick || boss Katana.bHitPending`.
     - **Stolen mechanics in the boss's hands**: `JamLoadoutRules::InHandsOf(Side, Balance)` returns a balance copy; for the boss `KatanaWindUp = BossKatanaWindUp`, katana/finisher/shuriken/explosion damage × `BossStolenDamage`, `ShurikenCooldown` and `DashCooldown` × `BossStolenCooldown`, `ShurikenSpeed` × `BossStolenSpeed`. `Step` uses a small `FHands { Hero ref, Boss copy, Of(Side) }` at the call sites `MoveFighter` (dash), `UseButtons`, `Land`, `Explode` (by `Done.Owner`). When the boss's press starts a strike he stands through the wind-up (via `IsBusy`), and after the finisher (strike 3) `Boss.BusyUntilTick = Katana.HitTick + Ticks(BossRecovery)` (a `JamBossRules` helper called from `UseButtons` when `bHitPending` turns true for the boss).
     - **Boss brain** (`Decide`, returns aim only when `Phase != Fight` or busy): owns Katana and hero within `KatanaRange + Hero.Radius` → `bPrimary`; owns Shuriken and hero beyond that reach → `bSecondary`; owns Dash and it is ready → dash away (`Move = -Aim.Dir`, return early) when a hero strike is pending and `InArc` would hit him, or dash at the hero when `Distance > DashDistance + SlamRange`. Tests: `Jam.Boss.SlashesWhenCloseAndOwnsKatana`, `ThrowsWhenFarAndOwnsShuriken`, `DashesOutOfAStrikeWhenOwnsDash`, `NeverUsesWhatHeDoesNotOwn`, `Jam.Fight.BossKatanaDamagesTheHero` (scaled damage, long wind-up), plus an `InHandsOf` test in `Jam.Loadout.*`.
     - **`JamCheckpointRules`** (new): `Capture(Fight, Balance)` = a fresh `JamFightRules::Start` with the boss health, ledger and `Steal.Done`/`Floor` of the fight (hero healed, start positions, nothing in the air); `Restore(Saved, Seed)` = copy with a fresh stream. Tests `Jam.Retry.RestoresBossHealthAtTheThreshold`, `HeroIsHealedAndKeepsOnlyUnstolenMechanics`, `ClearsProjectilesAndHazards`.
     - **Host**: `Checkpoint` member captured at `Restart` and whenever a step reports `StealResolved`; `Retry()` restores it with a fresh seed; `PendingChoice` latch (`if (HeroIntent.Choice >= 0) PendingChoice = ...`; each step consumes it) because a just-pressed key can fall on a frame with zero steps.
     - **Input**: controller sets `Intent.Choice` 0 for A / Left, 1 for D / Right on `WasInputKeyJustPressed` (always; the sim ignores it outside the card and before `OpenTick`). No mouse buttons for the card in K1 (note in the ticket). Game mode: R or Enter → `Retry` on `HeroDead`, `Restart` otherwise; F5 debug steal stays.
     - **HUD**: steal card (title, two option boxes "[A] NAME" / "[D] NAME", "No answer: he takes the left one", timer bar from `TimeLeft`), take-all card ("FINAL NOTE: CUT THE REST") also used when the offer has one option, stun banner, loss hint "R: retry from the last cut"; strings in a new `Public/UI/JamTexts.h` (`namespace JamTexts`, `inline const TCHAR* const`), ending titles moved there too; `DrawEnding` only for `HeroDead`/`Won`.
     - **Effects view**: the boss's blade sweep lasts his wind-up (`BossKatanaWindUp`, taken in `Init`) so the slow red blade is the telegraph; hero swings keep `SwingLife`.
     - **Demo**: drop the planned debug steals; the bot gives no choice so the card stays for its 3 seconds; `HeroDead` → `Retry` after 2.5 s, `Won` → `Restart`; new `-JamDemoCamera=<0|1|2>` to hold one camera for the per-camera clips.

     `Open`: `bTakesAll = Done >= ThresholdCount - 1`; empty offer → `++Done`, next floor, no card; else `JamFightOps::ClearStage`, `StartTick = Tick`, `OpenTick = Tick + Ticks(StealChoiceLock)`, `EndTick = Tick + Ticks(StealChoiceSeconds)`, `Phase = Steal`, event `StealOpened` (Side Boss, Amount = options). `Advance`: `Tick >= EndTick` → `Resolve(0)`; else `Tick >= OpenTick && Offer.IsValidIndex(Choice)` → `Resolve(Choice)`. `Resolve`: gives the picked option (or all with bTakesAll) to the boss through `JamLoadoutRules::Give` with one `StealResolved` event each, resets the offer, `++Done`, `Floor = ThresholdHealth(Done)`, `Phase = Fight`, `Boss.BusyUntilTick = Tick + Ticks(BossRecovery)`.

   **Edited in this segment:**
   - `Private/Sim/JamFightOps.cpp` (include `Sim/JamStealRules.h` added) and `Public/Sim/JamFightOps.h` (doc, `ClearStage` declared, Tests line gained `Jam.Steal.HealthStopsAtTheThreshold`):
     ```cpp
     bool JamFightOps::Hurt(FJamFight& Fight, EJamSide TargetSide, float Amount, const FVector2D& Pos, int32 ProjectileId)
     {
     	FJamFighter& Target = Fight.Fighters[TargetSide];
     	// Nothing hurts while the card is up or after the end, and a stunned boss cannot hurt back.
     	const bool bLive = Fight.Phase == EJamPhase::Fight || (Fight.Phase == EJamPhase::Stunned && TargetSide == EJamSide::Boss);
     	const bool bInvulnerable = !bLive || JamDashRules::IsInvulnerable(Target.Dash, Fight.Tick);
     	// The boss's health waits at the threshold of the next steal: one big hit never skips a cut.
     	const float Floor = TargetSide == EJamSide::Boss ? Fight.Steal.Floor : 0.f;
     	const float After = JamStealRules::ClampToThreshold(JamHealthRules::Apply(Target.Health, Amount, bInvulnerable), Floor);
     	// A floor above the health he has is not a heal.
     	Target.Health = FMath::Min(Target.Health, After);
     	if (bInvulnerable) { return false; }
     	AddEvent(Fight, EJamEvent::Hit, TargetSide, ProjectileId, Pos, Amount);
     	return true;
     }

     void JamFightOps::ClearStage(FJamFight& Fight)   // Projectiles.Reset, Hazards.Reset, bHitPending = false for both
     ```

4. Errors and fixes:

   - Caught while writing `Hurt`: clamping to the steal floor with `max` would heal a boss whose health a test set below the floor (for example 5 with a floor of 750). Fixed with `Target.Health = FMath::Min(Target.Health, After)`. Consequence for tests: a test that sets the boss's health low must also set `F.Steal.Floor = 0.f`, otherwise the next step opens a steal card (`BossHealth <= Floor`).
   - A local variable first named `Hurt` inside `JamFightOps::Hurt` was renamed to `After`.
   - Card clock semantics were settled to match the written tests: in the `Steal` phase the step does `++Tick` first and then `Advance`, with `Tick >= OpenTick` for an answer and `Tick >= EndTick` for the timeout (a card opened directly at tick 0 with a 30-step lock ignores 29 steps and accepts on the 30th; the timeout resolves on step 180 of 180).

Frame order owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`: intent (player or demo bot), `UJamFightSubsystem::Advance` (calls `JamFightRules::Play` per step, collects `FrameEvents`), sync views, camera, demo end-of-frame. No pawn; view target is `AJamCameraRig`.
   - Every mechanic (Katana, Shuriken, Dash) has one owner in `FJamLedger`; a steal is a change of owner. Hero attack buttons fall back to the weak ball; the boss gets nothing he was not given. The Publisher's brain (`JamBossRules::Decide`) writes the same intent a player writes.
   - Block C sim model: `EJamPhase { Fight, Steal, Stunned, HeroDead, Won }`; boss health is clamped at `Fight.Steal.Floor` (threshold of the next steal: 750/500/250 of 1000 in the fixture, then 0); `JamOutcomeRules::Settle` at the end of each step opens the card (`JamStealRules::Open`), stuns (everything returns to the hero, stage cleared, event `Stunned`) or sets HeroDead/Won; in `Steal` the step only runs the card clock (`++Tick` then `JamStealRules::Advance`: answer counts when `Tick >= OpenTick` and the choice is a valid index, timeout at `Tick >= EndTick` resolves option 0); the last card has `bTakesAll`; `Events` are reset at the start of every `Step` (a `Hurt` called outside a step is invisible to `Settle`); `Hurt` is live only in `Fight`, or in `Stunned` for the boss; stolen mechanics use `JamLoadoutRules::InHandsOf(Boss, Balance)` (longer katana wind-up, damage ×`BossStolenDamage`, shuriken speed ×`BossStolenSpeed`, cooldowns ×`BossStolenCooldown`); the boss stands through his katana wind-up (`IsBusy` includes `Katana.bHitPending` and the stun) and rests `BossRecovery` after his finisher; checkpoint = `JamCheckpointRules::Capture/Restore`.

Core of the cpp:
     ```cpp
     void UJamFightSubsystem::Restart()
     {
     	Balance = JamConfig::Load<FJamBalanceData>(JamConfig::BalanceSection);
     	// logs JamBalanceRules::Problems as errors
     	Begin(JamFightRules::Start(Balance, NewSeed()));
     	Checkpoint = JamCheckpointRules::Capture(Fight, Balance);
     }
     void UJamFightSubsystem::Retry() { Begin(JamCheckpointRules::Restore(Checkpoint, NewSeed())); }
     void UJamFightSubsystem::Advance(float FrameDt)
     {
     	const JamStepRules::FFrameSteps Frame = JamStepRules::StepsFor(Accumulated, FrameDt, JamSim::StepDt, MaxStepsPerFrame);
     	if (HeroIntent.Choice >= 0) { PendingChoice = HeroIntent.Choice; }
     	FJamIntent Intent = HeroIntent;
     	Intent.Choice = PendingChoice;
     	FrameEvents.Reset();
     	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Frame.Steps; ++Index)
     	{
     		JamFightRules::Play(Fight, Intent, Balance);
     		FrameEvents.Append(Fight.Events);
     		if (HasStealResolved(Fight.Events)) { Checkpoint = JamCheckpointRules::Capture(Fight, Balance); }
     	}
     	if (Frame.Steps > 0) { PendingChoice = -1; }
     	Accumulated = Frame.Remainder;
     	Alpha = Accumulated / JamSim::StepDt;
     }
     void UJamFightSubsystem::DebugSkip()   // in Fight phase: Boss.Health = Min(Boss.Health, Fight.Steal.Floor)
     ```

Questions that need his eyes go to `QUESTIONS.md`; do not stop the work for them: take the default and go on." Kirill delivers music, sound and UI through a Google Drive folder. Nobody but the agent touches git or the editor.
   - **The game**: NEON RONIN (working title). One arena, one enemy, the Publisher; a 3 to 4 minute boss fight. At boss health thresholds the Publisher grabs two random mechanics, the player picks which to give away, the boss uses it for the rest of the fight. Each steal strips a cosmetic layer. Below 20 percent the player is a bean with a weak ball shot; at 0 the boss is stunned, everything returns, the player lands the final blow. Win title "NEON RONIN: DIRECTOR'S CUT", loss "SHIPPED AS PROOF OF CONCEPT", retry from the last steal.
   - **Today**: "One session, no subagents, test first." Block A (done, `b013633`), Block B (done, `9986620`), Block C (done, `2a0c21b`). Then a bot playthrough, package Shipping, push to the itch draft, one clip per camera. If time is left: Parry and Ultimate. "Build from the bean up." "Damage, health and thresholds live in one data table." Playable build at 23:00 PC clock (UTC+2); the author picks a camera by Friday 07:00.
   - **Standing rules**: test first (`Jam.<Area>.<Case>` before the code, decisions in pure `*Rules`, pick the seams yourself). After each block: build and tests, commit, a 30 to 60 second clip, three lines to the author and to `DEVLOG.md`. "Never amend a commit. Package Shipping every evening. Warn before a run that opens a game window." Before a wave of subagents or any workflow: a forecast in chat (agents, tokens, share of the limit, a cheap variant) and the author's explicit yes. Third-party content goes to `THIRD_PARTY.md` when added; every AI tool used goes to the tool list in `DEVLOG.md`. **"Never print API keys, and keep the partner perk codes out of every file and log."** Hard checkpoints (PC clock, UTC+2): playable build Thu 23:00, test build Fri 18:00, feature freeze Sat 22:00, video Sun 18:00, submit Sun 20:00 and never later than 21:59.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unreal Engine 5.8 C++ project `JamGame` (engine at `F:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8`), automation tests `Jam.<Area>.<Case>` with `JamTest::Flags` (`IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FJamXxxTest, "Jam.Area.Case", JamTest::Flags)`, wrapped in `#if WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS`, including `Tests/JamTestHelpers.h`, with a file header comment naming the ticket, how a bug looks in game, and "Run: Automation RunTests Jam.X"). `TestEqual` works with the enum classes. Pure `namespace <Feature>Rules` with `JAMGAME_API` free functions and a header comment line `Tests: Jam.<Area>.*`. `AddInfo(...)` lines of a test appear in `Saved/BuildCycle/AutoTests_last.log` as `LogAutomationController: <text>`.
   - Code standard (`.claude/rules/cpp.md`): Epic style + `.clang-format` (`clang-format -i --style=file <files>`; tabs, Allman, ColumnLimit 140); English comments; folders `Public/<System>`, `Private/<System>`, `Private/Tests`; `Core` and `Sim` never include `View`, `UI`, `Audio`; `Game` includes `Sim`, `View`, `UI`; `View`/`UI` include `Core`/`Sim` only. Ratchet in `Tools/Quality/check_code.py`: file 600 lines, function 60 lines, nesting 3, no `LogTemp`, include cycles flagged. Balance numbers in data; sim randomness from the seeded stream; the number of `Jam.` tests only grows (baseline in `Tools/Quality/baseline.json` is **94** as committed; the working tree now has **100** tests; update with `python Tools/Quality/check_code.py --tests-log Saved/BuildCycle/AutoTests_last.log --update-baseline` after a full `Jam.` run).
   - Architecture: the fight is a pure fixed-step (60 Hz) simulation on plain data, `FJamFight`; flat 2D arena; fighters driven by `FJamIntent` (held-button semantics, `int32 Choice = -1` for the steal card). Frame order owned by `AJamGameMode::Tick`. No pawn; view target is `AJamCameraRig`. Every mechanic (Katana, Shuriken, Dash) has one owner in `FJamLedger`; a steal is a change of owner. Hero attack buttons fall back to the weak ball; the boss's brain (`JamBossRules::Decide`) writes the same intent a player writes.
   - Block C sim model: `EJamPhase { Fight, Steal, Stunned, HeroDead, Won }`; boss health is clamped at `Fight.Steal.Floor` (threshold of the next steal: `StealThreshold1..3` × `BossHealth`, then 0); `JamOutcomeRules::Settle` at the end of each step opens the card (`JamStealRules::Open`, which clears the stage and emits `StealOpened`), stuns (everything returns, stage cleared, event `Stunned`) or sets HeroDead/Won; in `Steal` the step only runs the card clock (answer counts when `Tick >= OpenTick`, timeout at `Tick >= EndTick` resolves option 0); the offer of a pair card is shuffled by the fight's stream (so "left" is a random mechanic); the last card has `bTakesAll`; `Resolve` emits one `StealResolved` (Side = Boss, Amount = mechanic) per mechanic taken and gives the boss a `BossRecovery` pause; `Events` are reset at the start of every `Step`; stolen mechanics use `JamLoadoutRules::InHandsOf(Boss, Balance)`; the boss stands through his katana wind-up (`JamBossRules::IsBusy` includes `Katana.bHitPending` and the stun) and rests `BossRecovery` after his finisher; checkpoint = `JamCheckpointRules::Capture/Restore`.
   - Balance and camera numbers: `USTRUCT`s loaded by reflection from flat ini sections `[JamBalance]` and `[JamCamera]` of `Config/DefaultGame.ini` through `JamConfig::Load<T>(Section, &Missing)` (`JamConfig::BalanceSection`). Tests of rules use the fixture `JamTest::Balance()` (round numbers); the full-fight bot tests use the real ini. Current game ini `[JamBalance]`: ArenaRadius 1500, HeroRadius 40, HeroSpeed 600, HeroHealth 100, BossRadius 70, BossSpeed 330, **BossHealth 4000 (uncommitted trial; committed value 1000)**, BallDamage 5, BallSpeed 1500, BallCooldown 0.35, BallRadius 14, BallLife 2.5, KatanaRange 230, KatanaHalfArc 65, KatanaWindUp 0.1, KatanaRecovery 0.16, KatanaChainWindow 0.45, KatanaStep 45, KatanaDamage 14, KatanaFinisherDamage 30, ShurikenDamage 9, ShurikenSpeed 2400, ShurikenCooldown 0.4, ShurikenRadius 16, ShurikenLife 1.1, ShurikenExplosiveEvery 3, ShurikenExplosionRadius 230, ShurikenExplosionDamage 22, DashDistance 480, DashDuration 0.18, DashInvulnerable 0.26, DashCooldown 0.9, MemoCount 5, MemoSpread 56, MemoSpeed 750, MemoDamage 8, MemoRadius 24, MemoLife 4.5, VolleyCooldown 3.2, SlamRange 520, SlamRadius 300, SlamTelegraph 0.9, SlamDamage 24, SlamCooldown 5, BossRecovery 0.9, StealThreshold1/2/3 0.75/0.5/0.25, StealChoiceSeconds 3, StealChoiceLock 0.6, BossStolenDamage 0.6, BossStolenCooldown 2, BossStolenSpeed 0.5, BossKatanaWindUp 0.45.
   - `JamSim::Ticks(Seconds)`, `JamSim::Other(Side)`, `TJamPerSide<T>`, `FJamIntents`, `JamSim::MechanicCount = 3`, `JamSim::StepsPerSecond = 60`, `JamStealRules::ThresholdCount = 3`.
   - Unity builds: names in anonymous namespaces must be unique across cpp files of the module. Already used (do not reuse): `CountEvents`, `TestCamera`, `KatanaOrigin`/`KatanaFacing`/`AtAngle`, `BossFight`/`NotSoon`, `MakeFighter`/`IsHeld`/`MoveFighter`/`AdvanceProjectiles`/`ShootBall`/`UseButton`/`UseButtons`/`HeroStartX`/`BossStartX`/`FHands`/`WaitForTheChoice`, `StrikesInSeries`, `StartSlam`/`StartVolley`/`WantsToDash`, `RangeSoftness`/`RangeOverSlam`/`CircleMargin`/`DodgeSeconds`/`DodgeMargin`/`CircleOver`/`IsAboutToBeHit`/`BladeMargin`/`IsUnderTheBlade`/`NoteTheCuts`, `MaxStepsPerFrame`/`NewSeed`/`HasStealResolved`, `DemoCameraSeconds`/`CaptureFps`/`DemoEndingSeconds`/`DemoCameras`, `HeroBeanColor`/`BossBeanColor`/`CameraName`, projectiles view: `FlightHeight`/`HeroShotColor`/`BossShotColor`/`ShurikenWidth`/`ShurikenThickness`/`ExplosiveColor`/`MemoSize`/`MemoRoll`/`MemoColor`/`SetShape`/`Dress`; effects view: `SwingLife`/`ExplosionLife`/`SlamLife`/`TrailLife`/`BladeHeight`/`BladeWidth`/`BladeThickness`/`FinisherScale`/`GroundThickness`/`CircleHeight`/`FillHeight`/`FlashHeight`/`TrailHeight`/`ExplosionStartSize`/`CircleColor`/`FillColor`/`SlamColor`/`ExplosionColor`/`HeroBladeColor`/`BossBladeColor`/`FinisherColor`/`HeroTrailColor`/`BossTrailColor`/`ClearsTheAir`; controller `LookAimReach`; arena view `FloorThickness`/`PostCount`/`PostWidth`/`PostHeight`/`FloorColor`/`PostColorA/B`/`GridStep`/`GridWidth`/`GridHeight`/`GridColor`/`SunRotation`; tests: `HeroHas`/`AnswerFor`, `OptionsOnACard`, `WasBossHit`/`Stun`, `ThiefFight`/`UsesStolenButtons`, `LastStretch`/`KnockDown`, `AfterTheFirstCut`/`PlayOnAndDie`, and in JamBotRunTest: `SeedsPerPolicy`/`RunLimitSeconds`/`ShortestMedianSeconds`/`LongestMedianSeconds`/`StealOrders`/`EveryPolicy`/`PolicyNames`/`GameBalance`/`RunPolicy`/`RunEveryPolicy`/`WinSeconds`/`MedianOf`/`TableRow`/`OrderOf`.

Step order in `JamFightRules::Step`: MoveFighter (sets `Facing` from `Intent.Aim.Dir`, sets `PrevPos`) for both, AdvanceProjectiles, hazards, UseButtons, `JamBossRules::Act` (StartSlam / StartVolley along `Boss.Facing`), katana Land, `JamOutcomeRules::Settle`, `++Tick`. A projectile starts at `Self.Pos + Dir * (Self.Radius + Shot.Radius)`. The only randomness in a fight is the shuffle of the steal offer (seeded stream), so fights differ only by which mechanics go in which order.
   - Boss brain: dash (stolen) out of a pending hero strike or when the hero is farther than `DashDistance + SlamRange`; slam when within `SlamRange` and ready; stolen katana when in blade reach; else volley when ready; else chase; stolen shuriken thrown whenever not in blade reach and not slamming. `IsBusy` = stunned, `Tick < BusyUntilTick`, or his katana pending.

     ```cpp
     /**
      * The direction in which a projectile thrown from From at Speed meets a target that keeps its course. HeadStart is how
      * far in front of the thrower the projectile appears. A target that cannot be caught is aimed at directly.
      */
     JAMGAME_API FVector2D LeadDirection(
     	const FVector2D& From, const FVector2D& TargetPos, const FVector2D& TargetVelocity, float Speed, float HeadStart);
     ```
   - `Private/Sim/JamBossRules.cpp`: in `Decide`, the volley branch now is
     ```cpp
     else if (Fight.Tick >= Fight.Boss.VolleyReadyTick)
     {
     	// The volley goes where the hero will be: running in one direction is not a way to dodge it.
     	const FVector2D HeroVelocity = (Hero.Pos - Hero.PrevPos) / JamSim::StepDt;
     ```

     and
     ```cpp
     FVector2D JamBossRules::LeadDirection(
     	const FVector2D& From, const FVector2D& TargetPos, const FVector2D& TargetVelocity, float Speed, float HeadStart)
     {
     	const FVector2D To = TargetPos - From;
     	const FVector2D Direct = To.GetSafeNormal();
     	// The two meet when |To + TargetVelocity * T| = HeadStart + Speed * T: a quadratic in T.
     	const double A = TargetVelocity.SizeSquared() - Speed * Speed;
     	const double B = 2.0 * (FVector2D::DotProduct(To, TargetVelocity) - Speed * HeadStart);
     	const double C = To.SizeSquared() - HeadStart * HeadStart;
     	if (A > -UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER || C <= 0.0)
     	{
     		// A target as fast as the projectile, or one already at the muzzle: no lead is worth guessing.
     		return Direct;
     	}
     	// A is negative and C positive: the roots have opposite signs, this one is the future.
     	const double Seconds = (-B - FMath::Sqrt(B * B - 4.0 * A * C)) / (2.0 * A);
     	return (To + TargetVelocity * Seconds).GetSafeNormal();
     }
     ```
   - `Private/Sim/JamBotRules.cpp`: constants `TurnSeconds = 1.5f`, `TurnMargin = 30.f`; new helpers
     ```cpp
     /** True when the fighter, moving on at Velocity, runs into an enemy projectile within the turn time. */
     bool CourseIsHit(const FJamFight& Fight, EJamSide Side, const FVector2D& Velocity)
     // for each enemy projectile: Gap = Flying.Pos - Me.Pos; Closing = Flying.Vel - Velocity;
     // Seconds = clamp(-Dot(Gap, Closing) / |Closing|^2, 0, TurnSeconds) (0 when Closing is zero);
     // hit when |Gap + Closing * Seconds| < Me.Radius + Flying.Radius + TurnMargin

     FVector2D CircleMove(const FJamFight& Fight, EJamSide Side, const FJamIntent& Kiting)
     {
     	const FJamFighter& Me = Fight.Fighters[Side];
     	const FVector2D Sideways(-Kiting.Aim.Dir.Y, Kiting.Aim.Dir.X);
     	const FVector2D Left = Kiting.Move;
     	const FVector2D Right = Left - 2.0 * FVector2D::DotProduct(Left, Sideways) * Sideways;
     	const bool bGoesRight = FVector2D::DotProduct(Me.Pos - Me.PrevPos, Sideways) < -UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER;
     	const FVector2D Course = bGoesRight ? Right : Left;
     	const FVector2D Turned = bGoesRight ? Left : Right;
     	const bool bTurn = CourseIsHit(Fight, Side, Course * Me.Speed) && !CourseIsHit(Fight, Side, Turned * Me.Speed);
     	return bTurn ? Turned : Course;
     }
     ```
     `Play` now does `Intent.Move = CircleMove(Fight, Side, Intent);` right after `Kite(...)`; `RunFight` sets `Run.BossHealthLeft = Fight.Fighters[EJamSide::Boss].Health;`.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - A Bash call with a heredoc was blocked by the `bash-guard` hook (my slip, a no-op call). Rule stands: no heredocs; multi-line content goes to a file with Write.
   - The first version of the head-start check in `Jam.Boss.LeadsARunningTarget` computed the meeting time wrongly; fixed before the build to `Seconds = 800.0 * Head.Y / Head.X / 300.0`.
   - The first geometry of `Jam.Bot.TurnsItsCircleBeforeAMemo` (boss at 700) gave the bot a radial move, so the head-on memo was not on its course; fixed by placing the boss at exactly the held range (`1.5 * B.SlamRange`).
   - Design catch: without the head start term the lead misses systematically (the memo appears `Boss.Radius + MemoRadius` ahead); `LeadDirection` takes `HeadStart`.
   - T-016 walkover/trap: with BossHealth 4000 the bot lost 26 of 26 fights where the Dash went early; cause was diving in without the dash; fixed with the "dive only with the dash" rule (60 of 60 wins).
   - Open: after T-033 the bot loses 60 of 60 (see Problem Solving).

5. Problem Solving:
   - T-016 done and committed (`9f42bb0`): 101 tests green, check_code 0 warnings. Finding: a circling bot was never hit (closest win at 74 health), the bean stage is the longest (1000 health at about 14 damage a second, about 70 s).
   - Shipping pipeline verified: the package builds (exit 0, `Saved/Packaged/NeonRonin_K1.zip`, 303 MB), and the packaged exe played 8 seconds with the bot (239 frames, contact sheet read: arena, fighters, slam circle, memos, HUD with "THE PUBLISHER x / 4000"). That package holds the T-016 code (commit `9f42bb0`, before T-033). Debug hint lines (F5/F6, camera keys) are visible in Shipping. The zip name is a placeholder: the team name is not known.
   - T-033 in progress, red: with the leading volley and the bot's circle turn, the full run is 102 pass, 2 fail (`Jam.Bot.EveryStealOrderIsWinnable`, `Jam.Bot.FightLengthIsInRange`). Bot table: wins 0 of 60 in every order; "lost 60 of 60 | died after: median 102 s | boss health left: median 750, from 340 to 1460". The fixture duel still reads "after a minute: hero 92 of 100, boss 0 of 1000". So the bot survives the first two stages and dies in the third or in the bean stage (below 1000), where it has no dash.
   - Hypotheses not yet tested: (a) the turn rule refuses to turn because the turned course runs into the low-angle memos of the same fan (`!CourseIsHit(Turned)` false), so without the dash the centre memo lands every volley (8 damage per 3.2 s over a 70 s bean stage is more than 100 health); (b) linear prediction over 1.5 s is poor on a circular path; (c) the bean stage is simply too long for a dashless hero under led volleys. Options: fix the turn rule (shorter horizon, turn whenever the course is hit, compare miss distances of the two courses, or step sideways through the fan's gaps), shorten the bean stage (raise BallDamage, lower thresholds), lower MemoDamage or raise VolleyCooldown, or lower BossHealth. Target: every order winnable, median won fight 120 to 300 s, wins that cost health (not a walkover, not impossible). If it cannot be made green quickly, the fallback is to keep the lead out of K1 (revert the `Decide` change or gate it) so the 23:00 build stays winnable.

   - Steals (`JamStealRules`): `ThresholdCount` 3 (StealThreshold1/2/3), `Offer` = shuffle of the hero's owned mechanics cut to 2 options (`OptionsOnACard`), the last steal (`Done >= ThresholdCount - 1`) takes all that is left; `Resolve` gives to the boss, raises `StealResolved` events, sets boss busy for BossRecovery.      // The stolen shuriken fills the gaps between his own attacks: it is thrown at a hero who keeps away. Never on

     ```cpp
     /** Seconds until a projectile ends on its own: at the arena wall or when its life runs out. */
     double SecondsToTheEnd(const FJamProjectile& Flying, int32 Tick, float ArenaRadius)
     // Life = max(DieTick - Tick, 0) * StepDt; Inside = ArenaRadius^2 - |Pos|^2; returns 0 when outside, Life when standing;
     // ToTheWall = (-Along + sqrt(Along^2 + SpeedSquared * Inside)) / SpeedSquared with Along = Dot(Pos, Vel); min(Life, ToTheWall)

     /** True when an explosive projectile goes off within the turn time next to where the fighter will be by then. */
     bool BlastReaches(const FJamFight& Fight, const FJamProjectile& Flying, EJamSide Side, const FVector2D& Velocity,
     	const FJamBalanceData& Balance)
     // Seconds = SecondsToTheEnd(...); Blast = Flying.Pos + Flying.Vel * Seconds; Mine = Me.Pos + Velocity * Seconds;
     // Seconds <= TurnSeconds && Distance(Blast, Mine) < Balance.ShurikenExplosionRadius + Me.Radius + TurnMargin

     bool CourseIsHit(const FJamFight& Fight, EJamSide Side, const FVector2D& Velocity, const FJamBalanceData& Balance)
     // per enemy projectile: explosive and BlastReaches -> true; else closest approach within TurnSeconds
     // closer than Me.Radius + Flying.Radius + TurnMargin -> true

     FVector2D CircleMove(const FJamFight& Fight, EJamSide Side, const FJamIntent& Kiting, const FJamBalanceData& Balance)
     // keeps the direction the fighter already circles in; turns when the course is hit and the turned one is not

     /** The move with the arena wall in mind: the closer the wall, the more the fighter turns back into the open. */
     FVector2D OffTheWall(const FJamFighter& Me, const FVector2D& Move, const FJamBalanceData& Balance)
     {
     	const double Room = WallRoomInBlasts * Balance.ShurikenExplosionRadius;
     	const double FromCentre = Me.Pos.Size();
     	// Nothing where the room begins, one where the fighter touches the wall.
     	const double Depth = FMath::Min((FromCentre + Me.Radius + Room - Balance.ArenaRadius) / Room, 1.0);
     	if (FromCentre < UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER || Depth <= 0.0)
     	{
     		return Move;
     	}
     	const FVector2D Outward = Me.Pos / FromCentre;
     	const FVector2D Pushed = (Move - Outward * (WallPush * Depth)).GetSafeNormal();
     	return Pushed.IsNearlyZero() ? -Outward : Pushed;
     }
     ```
     In `Play`: `Intent.Move = OffTheWall(Me, CircleMove(Fight, Side, Intent, Balance), Balance);` right after `Kite(...)` (only the kiting move is bent; the slam circle escape, blade retreat and the dive are untouched). `RunFight` records `Run.BossHealthLeft`.
